`generate_c_function()` / `generate_python_function()`: sinh ham C/Python\n     tu mo ta (`FunctionSpec`) THEO MAU CO SAN (cung phong cach voi\n     dataset/ch2_memory_bugs va dataset/ch4_fuzz_targets), phuc vu dung de\n     SINH DU LIEU HOC TAP (co the "tiem" loi bo nho pho bien: buffer-overflow,\n     memory-leak, double-free, use-after-free — dung cac ham nguy hiem/mau\n     loi kinh dien de sinh vien luyen phat hien, KHONG sinh ma khai thac).\n\n4) Phan loai CWE/CVE\n   - `CWE_TABLE`: anh xa moi loai loi (category) ma core.memory_analyzer,\n     core.static_smt, core.kripke phat hien sang ma CWE (Common Weakness\n     Enumeration) chuan.\n   - `ILLUSTRATIVE_CVE_TABLE`: mot vai vi du CVE cong khai, noi tieng, dung de\n     minh hoa cho tung loai CWE trong bai giang (KHONG chua chi tiet khai\n     thac — chi ten, nam va mo ta tong quat da duoc cong bo rong rai).\n\n5) Pipeline tong hop: Specs -> Kripke -> Code -> CWE/CVE -> Ch1-5\n   - `run_pipeline()`: chay TOAN BO luồng xu ly tren mot `Specification`:\n       (a) kiem chung Kripke/CTL (Chuong 1)\n       (b) sinh ma C tu dac ta va quet bang static checker (Chuong 2)\n       (c) trich xuat AST/dieu kien + kiem tra SAT/tran so bang Z3, VA SINH\n           TEST CASE TU DONG (symbolic execution + Z3) cho cac ham Python\n           sinh tu dac ta (Chuong 3)\n       (d) sinh ham Python tu dac ta va chay black-box/concolic fuzzing,\n           DOI CHIEU voi bo test sinh tu SMT o (c) tren CUNG cac ham nay\n           (Chuong 4)\n       (e) phan loai LUONG xu ly (RAII/type-safety/exception-safety/...)\n           tren ma C sinh ra bang bo phan loai cua Chuong 5\n       (f) tong hop toan bo phat hien va phan loai theo CWE/CVE (Chuong 6)\n     Tra ve `PipelineReport` co `to_text()` de in bao cao day du TU CHUONG\n     1 DEN CHUONG 5, cong voi phan tong hop CWE/CVE cua chinh Chuong 6.\n')
               STORE_NAME               1 (__doc__)
